!function () {
  // (c) https://github.com/burocratik/outdated-browser
  var onLoad = window.onload;
  window.onload = function () {
    try { if (typeof onLoad === 'function') { onLoad(); } } catch (ex) {}
    var wrapper = document.getElementById('outdated-browser');
    var updateButton = document.getElementById('outdated-browser-update');
    var closeButton = document.getElementById('outdated-browser-close');
    var reactRoot = document.getElementById('react-root');

    var cssomPrefixes = 'Moz O ms Webkit'.split(' ');
    var modElem = {
      elem: document.createElement('modernizr')
    };
    var mStyle = {
      style: modElem.elem.style
    };

    function cssToDOM(name) {
      return name.replace(/([a-z])-([a-z])/g, function(str, m1, m2) {
        return m1 + m2.toUpperCase();
      }).replace(/^-/, '');
    }

    function domToCSS(name) {
      return name.replace(/([A-Z])/g, function(str, m1) {
        return '-' + m1.toLowerCase();
      }).replace(/^ms-/, '-ms-');
    }

    function testProps(props, prefixed, value, skipValueTest) {
      skipValueTest = typeof skipValueTest === 'undefined' ? false : skipValueTest;
      var afterInit, i, propsLength, prop, before;
      var elems = ['modernizr', 'tspan'];
      while (!mStyle.style) {
        afterInit = true;
        mStyle.modElem = document.createElement(elems.shift());
        mStyle.style = mStyle.modElem.style;
      }
      function cleanElems() {
        if (afterInit) {
          delete mStyle.style;
          delete mStyle.modElem;
        }
      }
      propsLength = props.length;
      for (i = 0; i < propsLength; i++) {
        prop = props[i];
        before = mStyle.style[prop];
        if (!!~('' + prop).indexOf('-')) {
          prop = cssToDOM(prop);
        }
        if (typeof mStyle.style[prop] !== 'undefined') {
          if (!skipValueTest && typeof value !== 'undefined') {
            try {
              mStyle.style[prop] = value;
            } catch (e) {}
            if (mStyle.style[prop] != before) {
              cleanElems();
              return true;
            }
          } else {
            cleanElems();
            return true;
          }
        }
      }
      cleanElems();
      return false;
    }

    function testAllProps(prop, value, skipValueTest) {
      var ucProp = prop.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + prop.slice(1);
      var props = (prop + ' ' + cssomPrefixes.join(ucProp + ' ') + ucProp).split(' ');
      return testProps(props, value, skipValueTest);
    }

    var supports = {
      flexbox: testAllProps('flexBasis', null, '1px'),
      flexboxLegacy: testAllProps('boxDirection', null, 'reverse'),
      flexboxTweener: testAllProps('flexAlign', null, 'end'),
      flexboxWrap: testAllProps('flexWrap', null, 'wrap'),
      transform: testAllProps('transform', null, 'rotate(0deg)'),
      functionBind: !!((function () {}).bind)
    };

    var supportsAnyFlexbox = (
      supports.flexbox
      || supports.flexboxLegacy
      || supports.flexboxTweener
    );

    var isOutdated = (
      !supportsAnyFlexbox
      || !supports.flexboxWrap
      || !supports.transform
      || !supports.functionBind
    );

    if (isOutdated) {
      reactRoot.style.visibility = 'hidden';
      wrapper.style.display = 'block';
      if (navigator.userAgent.indexOf('MSIE 6') > -1) {
        wrapper.style.position = 'absolute';
      }
      if (navigator.userAgent.indexOf('Mobile') > -1) {
        updateButton.style.display = 'none';
      }
      else {
        updateButton.onmouseover = function () {
          updateButton.style.color = '#f25648';
          updateButton.style.backgroundColor = '#ffffff';
        };
        updateButton.onmouseout = function () {
          updateButton.style.color = '#ffffff';
          updateButton.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent';
        };
      }
      closeButton.onclick = function () {
        wrapper.style.display = 'none';
        reactRoot.style.visibility = '';
      };
    }
  };
}();
